configure spanning-tree enable

   Enable the Spanning Tree Protocol.

configure spanning-tree forward-delay VALUE

   Set spanning-tree forward-delay timer in seconds. VALUE must be a value in
   range 4-30.

configure spanning-tree hello-time VALUE

   Set spanning-tree hello-time timer in seconds. VALUE must be a value in
   range 1-10.

configure spanning-tree max-age VALUE

   Set spanning-tree max-age timer in seconds. VALUE must be a value in range
   6-40.

configure spanning-tree mode MODE

   Set MODE to stp in order to use the old Spanning Tree algorithm; use rapid-
   stp (default) for the modern Rapid Spanning Tree algorithm.

configure spanning-tree priority VALUE

   Set spanning-tree priority value. VALUE must be a value in range 0-61440, in
   steps of 4096.

configure spanning-tree tx-hold-count VALUE

   Set spanning-tree tx-hold-count value. VALUE must be a value in range 0-10.
   Setting this to 0, disables any check on BPDU sending frequency.

configure svi-interfaces status dynamic

   Enable dynamic status management of SVI interfaces.

configure system USER password SECRET

   Change the password for the given user. USER can be admin, operator or
   user.

configure system authentication USER local

   Use credentials from local database for authentication of the given user.
   USER can be admin, operator or user.

configure system authentication USER radius

Consist Switch User Manual ver. 2.0.4  239/253
CLI commands reference

     Use credentials from configured RADIUS for authentication of the given
     user. USER can be admin, operator or user.

 configure system authentication USER radius local

     Use credentials at first from configured RADIUS and then fallback to local
     database for authentication of the given user. USER can be admin, operator
     or user.

 configure system clock source PROTOCOL ADDRESS [ADDRESS2]

     Set time auto-synchronization sources for given protocol. PROTOCOL must
     be one of ntp and timep. ADDRESS must be the valid IPv4 address of a
     network resource which provides synchronization service with the given
     protocol. The additional ADDRESS2 is optional.

 configure system hostname NAME

     Set the system host name.

 configure system logging debug SUBSYSTEMS

     Enable recording of the log messages in the applog. SUBSYSTEMS is the
     comma-separated list of subsystems for which the recording is enabled.

 configure system logging host ADDRESS

     Enable recording of the log messages in the remote syslog server at the
     given IP ADDRESS.

 configure system login banner TEXT

     Set the given TEXT as login banner for the system shell.

 configure system login failed-attempts VALUE

     Enable failed logins counting. Lock the account after the login failed the
     given number of times.

 configure system password complexity enable

     Enable password complexity checking.

  configure system radius-server primary ADDRESS SECRET

     Configure a primary RADIUS server for use by local user authentication.

  configure system radius-server primary options opt1=val1[,opt2=val2...]

     Configure options to use for the primary RADIUS server. Option optX could
     be: auth_port, acct_port, retry_cnt, timeout, src_ipaddr

  configure system radius-server backup ADDRESS SECRET

     Configure a backup RADIUS server for use by local user authentication.

  configure system radius-server backup options opt1=val1[,opt2=val2...]

     Configure options to use for the backup RADIUS server. Option optX could
     be: auth_port, acct_port, retry_cnt, timeout, src_ipaddr

 configure system timezone ZONE SITE

240/253  Consist Switch User Manual ver. 2.0.4
                                                                            CLI commands reference

   Set the current timezone. For valid values of ZONE and SITE arguments, see
   online help.

configure tcds address composition mask MASK

   MASK is a special netmask used by TCDS in the computation of the position-
   dependent addresses; its bits are set in the portion of the IP address to be
   replaced by the position value.

configure tcds backbone-vlan VID

   Set the Virtual Backbone VLAN identifier.

configure tcds enable

   Enable the Train Composition Discovery Service.

configure tcds lock reboot after VALUE minutes

   Configure timeout for rebooting after a composition lock; VALUE is
   expressed in minutes and 0 means do not reboot.

configure tcds mode plain|routed

   Select the TCDS mode of operations: plain if the same IP network is used for
   all the train, routed if a different IP network is used on each consist.

configure tcds ttcmp forwarding

   Enable the forwarding of TTCMP packets entering the consist boundaries.

configure tcds consist-network ADDRESS/MASK

   Virtual TBN configuration: consist network IP ADDRESS in use.

configure tcds enable [no-nat]

   Enable Virtual TBN role. Optionally the backbone NAT for consist networks
   can be disabled specifying the no-nat argument.

configure tcds ip-address-template TEMPLATE

   Template used for building VTBN addresses.

configure tcds mroute IN-IF SRC-ADDR MC-ADDR

   Add a IPv4 multicast route through the virtual backbone for traffic entering
   IN-IF and having multicast address MC-ADDR. SRC-ADDR can be the IPv4
   address of the sender or 0.0.0.0 for any address.

configure tcds train-network-template TEMPLATE

   Template used for building consist backbone addresses.

configure ttcmp auto confirm enable

   Enable automatic confirmation of TTCMP project when all other devices are
   confirmed.

configure ttcmp auto cst-id enable

   Enable automatic Consist-ID detection by TTCMP.

     Delete the TTCMP© project identified by ID.

     Remove the startup configuration from persistent storage.

 sysadmin load URL

     Load update data from the given URL.

  sysadmin port-mirror destination IF-NAME source IF-LIST

     Enable packet mirroring from all the interfaces specified in the comma-
     separated list IF-LIST to the destination interface IF-NAME.

  sysadmin reboot

     Reboot the system.

 sysadmin reset coach-id

     Reset the coach identifier value and description.

 sysadmin reset composition head

     Reset the Composition Head flag.

 sysadmin reset composition lock

     Reset composition discovery lock status.

 sysadmin reset cst-id

     Reset the current consist id value.

  sysadmin reset global project

     Reset the T TCMP© project on all the devices of the network.

  sysadmin reset local project

     Reset the T TCMP© project on the local device.

  sysadmin reset poe cache

     Clear the PoE cache.

 sysadmin save configfile URL [external|plain]

     Save current system configuration on the given network resource specified
     by URL. If external argument is present, the format will be suitable for
     external configuration mode. If plain argument is present, the configuration

246/253  Consist Switch User Manual ver. 2.0.4
                                       CLI commands reference

   will not be encapsulated in a KAD container.

sysadmin save image URL

   Save current system image on the given network resource specified by URL.

sysadmin save log URL [plain]

   Save current system logs on the given network resource specified by URL. If
   plain argument is present, the configuration will not be encapsulated in a
   KAD container.

sysadmin save tech-info URL [plain]

   Save technical data on the given network resource specified by URL. If plain
   argument is present, the configuration will not be encapsulated in a KAD
   container.

sysadmin set coach-id VALUE [description DESCRIPTION]

   Set the coach numeric identifier and an optional coach description.

sysadmin set composition head

   Set the Composition Head flag.

sysadmin set composition lock [direct|forced|reversed]

   Lock the composition discovery. If no argument is present, the lock direction
   takes into account the Composition head flag. If direct argument is present,
   the lock direction is the same of the discovered composition. If reversed
   argument is present, the lock direction is opposite to the discovered
   composition. If forced argument is present, the lock numbering starts from
   the local consist.

sysadmin set composition unlock

   Unlock the composition discovery.

sysadmin set cst-id VALUE

   Set the consist identifier as string (not exceeding 15 characters).

sysadmin set cst-id uuid/VALUE

   Set the consist identifier as UUID; the value must be a valid UUID parsable
   using the uuid_parse system function.

sysadmin set default image NAME

   Set the firmware image the system will load on next boot.

sysadmin set global project ID

   Set the given T TCMP© project to be used on all the devices of the network.

sysadmin set time DAY MONTH YEAR HOURS MINUTES [SECONDS]

   Set current system time. All arguments must be integer values. YEAR must
   be a value between 2010 and 2050.

sysadmin set topology match-mode default|default-fast|diffusion|none

Consist Switch User Manual ver. 2.0.4  247/253
CLI commands reference

     Select a T TCMP© project matching algorithm.

 sysadmin set topology status confirmed

     Confirm the current T TCMP© project.

 sysadmin set watchdog VALUE

     Set the configuration watchdog feature activation time in minutes. If VALUE
     is 0, the watchdog is deactivated.

  sysadmin show images

     Show firmware images currently stored in persistent storage.

 sysadmin show port-mirror

     Report active port mirror setup.

  sysadmin show projects

     List the T TCMP© projects currently stored in the device.

  sysadmin show watchdog

     Show the configuration watchdog status.

     NOTE

     Please refer to VDS Rail representatives for further information about
     Konuendo Consist Network Concept (KCNC) and the Train Topology and
     Configuration Management Protocol (T TCMP©).

250/253                     Consist Switch User Manual ver. 2.0.4
                                           Unavailable services in 7.2.x firmware series

Annex A - Unavailable services in
            7.2.x firmware series

A.1 Consist Switch product models equipped
with 19 or 28 Ethernet ports

The Consist Switch devices equipped with 19 or 28 ports work only with a firmware
version 7.0.0 or greater.

The services listed below are, at present time, not available on firmware series 7.2.x:

· Port configuration:                  · Coupled-Switch© mode

 Fast link detection                   · Link aggregation:

 MDI/MDI-X and flow control             LACP

 802.1x                                 distributed

 PoE mode `as-link'                    · Diagnostics data over IPTCom
                                           protocol

These services and functionalities will be added gradually to the next releases.

NOTE

The commands for the services or configurations listed above, even if not
effective, may be available anyway in firmware series 7.2.x.

Consist Switch User Manual ver. 2.0.4                                             251/253
